- **Evidence:** `MantleSetupData.xml:404-405` `<moqui.security.UserGroupPreference userGroupId="ALL_USERS" preferenceKey="mantle.content.root" preferenceValue="dbresource://mantle/content"/>`
- **Evidence:** `SmithySite.js:907` `String(ec.user.getPreference("mantle.content.root") || "dbresource://mantle/content").replace(/\/+$/, "") +`; `:1077-1078` `const location = assetRoot(p.siteId) + "/" + asset.assetId + "/" + fileNameOf(name)` / `ec.resource.getLocationReference(location).copyFrom(attachment.location)`
- **Evidence:** `ProjectServices.xml:1804-1806` `const root = String(ec.user.getPreference('mantle.content.root') || ...)` ... `ec.resource.getLocationReference(location).putText(String(notesText || ''))`
- **Evidence:** `tools.rs:2440` `let written = with_ec(|ec| ec.factory().resource().write_bytes(&to, &bytes))?;`
- **Evidence:** `resource.rs:1010-1015` `"file" | "runtime" | "component" => { let resource = self.get(location)?; resource.file_path() ...` and `:1057` `let path = super::locate::file_path(strip_hash(location))?;`
- **Evidence:** `crates/moqui-git/src/lib.rs:1829-1837` `let (repo, path) = self.located(location)?; Self::writable(&repo, location)?; ... self.put_file(&repo, location, &path, blob, note)`
- **Not confirmed:** not run. That a PASS account's session on the business host passes the screen walk to `/apps/setPreference` rests on the Routes and PASS maps, not on code read; whether a changed script is read again at once or on restart; whether a `git://` write to a repository Smithy does not keep starts a trusted run; which of the other Mantle and BOSS services that root their files at the same preference a PASS page or a BOSS user reaches. Looked for and not found: a check of the root in `assetRoot`, `addReleaseAsset` or `store#ReleaseNotes`; a production restriction of `file`, `runtime` and `component` writes in `write_bytes` or `writable_path` (only the entity fence, `resource.rs:531`); an authorization check in the `git` provider's `write_bytes_noted`; a refusal of the key in `set_preference` (`preference.rs:136-141` refuses only theme appearance keys); a process property that sets the preference (none in the conf). The forum gate the notes pass refuses words and links, not paths, and the repository's Owner moderates its own scope (`SmithySite.js:744`).
- **Suggested fix:** Take the content root from a conf setting (or a `UserGroupPreference` the user cannot override), never from `UserPreference`, in every service that writes under it; meanwhile refuse `mantle.content.*` keys in `set_preference`. In `assetRoot` and `store#ReleaseNotes` refuse a root that is not `dbresource://` (or the configured root) or that holds `#`, `?` or `..`. Make the resource facade refuse a location with `#` for a write.
- **Re-check:** Read `webroot/screen/webroot/apps.xml:15-28` (`require-authentication="false"`; `setPreference` checks only `ec.user.userId` before `ec.user.setPreference(preferenceKey, value)`); `crates/moqui-core/src/user/preference.rs:130-155` (`set_preference`: any key, refused only for an appearance key a theme locks) and `:55-72` (`get_preference_for`: a process property of the key's name wins, else the user's row, else a group's; no conf sets `mantle.content.root`, mr-mantle `MoquiConf.xml:4-8` only names it); mr-mantle `work/ProjectServices.xml:1804-1806` (`store#ReleaseNotes`: with no stored notes the location is `<root>/work/<workEffortId>/release-notes.md` and `putText(notesText)` writes the caller's text there), `smithy/SmithySite.js:826-830` (called when a release is stored with `notes`), `:906-910` (`assetRoot`) and `:1077-1078` (release files under the same root); `crates/moqui-core/src/context/resource.rs:1056-1080` (`file_ref`, `runtime_ref` and `component_ref` all `strip_hash(location)`) and `:1008-1019` (`writable_path` writes `file`, `runtime` and `component` locations). Not read by the lead: `storeRelease`'s own caller check (the reviewer: a Maintainer, which a self-registered account is of its own repository); whether a PASS public account can sign in on the server's web to reach `/apps/setPreference`, or any other path that sets a preference; `git://` roots (`crates/moqui-git/src/lib.rs:1829-1837`). The lead counted 37 files in mr-mantle and mr-boss services that read `mantle.content.root` (44 uses).

#### SEC-BOS-81. A treasury user's NACHA return file declines or re-authorizes any payment of any organization by its id, which unapplies and reverses it (SEC-BAP-08's fix does not reach `import#NachaReturn`)

High; confirmed (lead); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-18 (mr-mantle `0af37976`, `dead45d1`): `import#NachaReturn` skips a return entry whose payment is not found (or whose `X` id matches more than one), is not an ACH payment of the bank account (paid from it or received into it by its owner), is not in the `paymentIdList` given, or differs from the entry's amount; each skip is logged by payment id and trace number and the other entries go on. A notification of change (`98`, a zero amount) gets the same checks but the amount, is recorded once as a gateway response with its change code and corrected data, and no longer moves a settled payment back to authorized. With authorization on it asks `check#BankAccess` for `reconcile`, or `runApprove` for a payment run approver, as the other bank files do; the bank's `consume#NachaReturnSystemMessage` runs without a user, held to the bank account's payments. `importReturn` passes the payments of the organization's executed runs on the run's bank account, calls the import with authorization on, and needs `BANK_RECONCILE` or `PAYMENT_RUN_APPROVE` (as does `canImportReturn`). `generate#NachaFile` and the import both use `X` plus the rightmost 14 characters of a long id (`import#NachaReturn`, `generate#NachaFile`, `ext/account/NachaServices.xml`; `importReturn`, `account/PaymentRuns.js`). Tests: `AccountingPaymentRunControls` "a return file declines only the bank account's run payments at their amounts, and imported again changes nothing"; `BankOutputFiles` "import a NACHA return: returns decline their payments, the others are skipped, a change is recorded, a bad line is an error" and "the bank file services refuse a caller without finance authority in the bank account's organization".
- **Where:** `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Accounting/Payables/PaymentRunDetail.xml:91-102`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentRunServices.xml:246-258`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentRuns.js:761-800`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/NachaServices.xml:508-650`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/AccountingPayment.secas.xml:45-50`, `:78-83`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/AccountingLedger.secas.xml:68-72`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/AccountingFinancial.secas.xml:63-67`. Report: round 3 `D5.md`.
- **Reached by:** a signed-in member of `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_TREASURY`, `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_PAYROLL` or `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` (each holds `PAYMENT_EDIT` and has ALWAYS on Payables, `BossAccountingSetupData.xml:248`, `:274`, `:279`), by a POST with their own session token to `/qapps/Accounting/Payables/PaymentRunDetail/importReturn` naming any executed ACH payment run of their organization, with a crafted `returnText` (or `returnFile`).
- **What happens:** `importReturn` checks only that the run is the caller's organization's, under `PAYMENT_EDIT`, ACH and executed, and passes the caller's text to `import#NachaReturn` with authorization disabled. That service takes each entry record's payment id from columns 40-54 as written; for a last addenda of type `99` it finds the payment by that id alone and sets it `PmntDeclined`, for type `98` `PmntAuthorized`. Nothing compares the payment with the run, the bank account, the organization, the instrument or the amount; only an id beginning with `X` is looked up within the bank account. A delivered payment moved to Declined or Authorized runs the SECAs `PaymentUnDeliverCleanup` (`void#Payment`, which unapplies every application, so the paid invoices are open again), `PaymentUndeliverReverGlPost` (reverses the cash posting), `PaymentReverseFinAccountTx` (reverses a financial account movement) and `RunPaymentReturned`. So a finance user of organization A declines another organization's customer receipts or supplier payments, reopening their invoices and reversing their ledger. The answer counts only payments of A's own runs, so the screen says "0 payments returned". SEC-BAP-08's fix gave `generate#NachaFile` and the OFX and BAI imports `check#BankAccess`, not this service; its only other caller is `consume#NachaReturnSystemMessage`, the bank's channel.
- **Evidence:** `PaymentRunDetail.xml:94-95` `<service-call name="mantle.account.PaymentRunServices.import#RunReturn"` with `in-map="{paymentRunId:paymentRunId, organizationPartyId:organizationPartyId, nachaString:nachaString}"`
- **Evidence:** `PaymentRuns.js:762` `const run = runScope(p, "PAYMENT_EDIT")`; `:763` `if (run.paymentInstrumentEnumId !== "PiAch" || !run.executeOperationKey)`; `:785-787` `.name("mantle.ext.account.NachaServices.import#NachaReturn")` `.parameters({paymentMethodId: run.paymentMethodId, nachaString: p.nachaString})` `.disableAuthz()`
- **Evidence:** `NachaServices.xml:559` `paymentId: checkPaymentId(line.slice(39, 54).trim()),`; `:522` `if ("X" !== paymentId[0]) {` (the id returned unchecked); `:611` `const payment = ec.entity.find("mantle.account.payment.Payment").condition({paymentId: paymentId}).one()`; `:625` `.parameters({paymentId: paymentId, statusId: "PmntDeclined"}).call()`; `:643` `.parameters({paymentId: paymentId, statusId: "PmntAuthorized"}).call()`
- **Evidence:** `AccountingPayment.secas.xml:48-50` `statusChanged && oldStatusId == 'PmntDelivered' && ['PmntPromised', 'PmntAuthorized', 'PmntDeclined'].includes(statusId)` calls `mantle.account.PaymentServices.void#Payment`; `PaymentServices.xml:1294` `unapply#PaymentApplication` for each application; `AccountingAccountEntities.xml:1591` `statusId="PmntDelivered" toStatusId="PmntDeclined"`
- **Not confirmed:** not run. `revert#PaymentPost` and `reverse#PaymentTrans` were not read. Whether a `98` notice that puts a manually recorded ACH payment back to `PmntAuthorized` makes a scheduled `generate#NachaFile` job (a supported setup, `ZbaOrganizationDemoData.xml:323-328`) send it to the bank again was not traced. Looked for and not found: an organization, bank account or run check of each named payment in `import#NachaReturn` (but the `X` prefix lookup); a check after the call that the declined payments are the run's (the code only counts them, `PaymentRuns.js:766-778`, `:796`); a permission in `import#NachaReturn`. The entity filter sets of the Payables ALWAYS rows do not apply, as the call disables authorization.
- **Suggested fix:** In `importReturn` pass the run's payment ids (or the bank account's executed-run payment ids) and have `import#NachaReturn` refuse an entry whose payment is not among them, whose `paymentMethodId` is not the bank account or whose amount differs; give `import#NachaReturn` its own `check#BankAccess` (`reconcile` or `runApprove`) when authorization is on, as SEC-BAP-08's fix did for the other bank files, and call it with authorization on.
- **Re-check:** The lead read `account/PaymentRuns.js:780-788` (`importReturn` calls `import#NachaReturn` with the run's payment method and the uploaded text, `.disableAuthz()`), `ext/account/NachaServices.xml:555-560` (each entry's `paymentId` comes from the file) and `:606-628`, `:640-646` (the `Payment` is found by id alone and updated to `PmntDeclined` for a return or `PmntAuthorized` for a correction, with no test of the run, the bank account or the organization). Not read by the lead: the unapply and reversal past the status change.

#### SEC-BOS-82. Accounting's customer and supplier scope admits any related party, so AR, AP and treasury users read and change employees' identification numbers (an SSN among them) and payroll bank accounts

High; confirmed (lead); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-18 (mr-mantle `f3e8e5ab`, `5a173b93`, `3a673d16`; mr-boss `fbba666e`, `299c0fea`, `2fc3bfce`), employees taken out of Accounting's scope by the owner's decision: `check#CustomerScope` counts only `PrtCustomer` relationships and leaves out an employee's payroll invoices (those naming an employment), so a pay run's third-party payees stay suppliers, and it refuses an internal organization (`INTERCOMPANY_EXCLUDED`) and a party with a current `PrtEmployee` employment at any internal organization (`EMPLOYEE_EXCLUDED`); a contact recorded as employed by an outside company is still admitted. `store#CustomerBankAccount` refuses a `PmpPayroll` account. Payroll owns an employee's payroll bank accounts through People: `store#PayrollBankAccount` (`humanres/PayrollAssignments.js`, `PayrollSetupServices.xml`), used by a payroll editor of the employer on Pay assignments, adds one or changes a `PmpPayroll` one; it refuses a person an internal employer outside the editor's scope employs now (`EMPLOYED_ELSEWHERE`) and an ended employment with no unpaid payroll invoice (`EMPLOYMENT_ENDED`), tells the person (`PayrollBankAccountChanged`, in the app and by email, never the number), and the audit log records who set an account's purpose and who ended one. A second person's confirmation of a payroll bank change was considered and is not wanted (the owner's decision, 2026-11-18). `get#CustomerFinance`, `get#SupplierFinance` and Customer Service's customer read answer identifications through `CustomerFinance.js` `identifications`: never `PtidSsn`, `PtidFein` or `PtidFuid`, each value masked to its last four characters; `store#CustomerIdentification` refuses those types and a masked value, and the BOSS finance details show the masked value and start an edit empty. Marble's `EditParty`, `EditCustomer` and `EditSupplier` still show identifications whole under Marble's own groups. Tests: `AccountingCustomerFinance` "AR stores a customer identification and reads it masked; a tax identification is never answered nor written" and "an employee, an internal organization and a non-customer relationship are no customer or supplier of finance"; `PayAssignments` "a payroll editor adds and changes an employee's payroll bank account; others are refused, numbers never answered"; mr-boss `PeoplePayAssignments`.
- **Where:** `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/CustomerFinanceServices.xml:27-91` (`check#CustomerScope`), `:95-283` (`get#CustomerFinance`), `:285-323` (`store#CustomerIdentification`), `:369-416` (`store#CustomerBankAccount`); `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/SupplierServices.xml:131-237` (`get#SupplierFinance`); `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentMethodServices.xml:566-610` (`update#BankAccount`); `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/template/AccountingFinanceDetails.xml:24-72`, `:351-354`. Report: round 3 `D6.md`.
- **Reached by:** a holder of `RECEIVABLE_VIEW` and `RECEIVABLE_EDIT` (`BOSS_ACCOUNTING_AR`), of `PAYABLE_VIEW` and `PAYABLE_EDIT` (`BOSS_ACCOUNTING_AP`), or of `PAYMENT_EDIT` and `BANK_RECONCILE` (`BOSS_ACCOUNTING_TREASURY`, `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN`), none of whom holds a People or payroll permission, through `/qapps/Accounting/Receivables/Customer360/FinanceDetails` and `/qapps/Accounting/Payables/Supplier360/FinanceDetails` (the page and its `saveIdentification` and `saveFinanceBank` transitions) with `customerPartyId` or `supplierPartyId` from the request.
- **What happens:** `check#CustomerScope` admits a party as the organization's customer when any current `PartyRelationship` joins the two, whatever its type, and as its supplier when any invoice runs from the party to the organization. An employment is a `PrtEmployee` relationship from the person to the employer, and every paid employee has payroll invoices from them to the employer (`humanres/PayrollObligations.js:330-339`), so every employee passes both. `get#CustomerFinance` and `get#SupplierFinance` then answer the party's contacts and, to a holder of the side's edit permission, every `PartyIdentification` row with `idValue` decrypted; the Finance details tab shows it with `<display/>`. Payroll keeps the employee's SSN there (`PtidSsn`, `humanres/PayrollDocumentServices.xml:58`), which People itself never shows: `Employees.js` leaves `PtidSsn`, `PtidFein` and `PtidFuid` out even for HR and masks the rest to four digits. `store#CustomerIdentification` writes any `PartyIdType` of the party. `store#CustomerBankAccount` adds or changes the party's bank accounts; `update#BankAccount` clones an outside party's payment method with its new numbers, so a changed payroll account keeps `purposeEnumId` `PmpPayroll`, which `PaymentRuns.js` `supplierBank` pays first. People says employees' bank accounts are "changed through Accounting's payment-method services" (`PayrollAssignments.js:1026`), so a finance role may be meant to change them, but here every employee is reached under treasury authority with no payroll permission. A `PrtOrgRollup` row is also a current relationship: a user of a child organization names its parent as the customer and, the parent being internal, `update#BankAccount` changes the parent's own bank account in place, and `store#CustomerIdentification` writes the parent's identifications (its FEIN).
- **Evidence:** `CustomerFinanceServices.xml:76-84` `<entity-find-count entity-name="mantle.party.PartyRelationship" count-field="relationshipCount" ...><date-filter/>` with `<econdition field-name="fromPartyId" from="customerPartyId"/>` and `toPartyId` the organization, no `relationshipTypeEnumId` condition; `:53` `<econdition field-name="fromPartyId" from="supplierSide ? customerPartyId : organizationPartyId"/>` (any invoice type counts)
- **Evidence:** `CustomerFinanceServices.xml:128` `canEditIdentifications:held.RECEIVABLE_EDIT`; `:275-277` `<entity-find entity-name="mantle.party.PartyIdentification" list="identifications" limit="100" disable-authz="true"><econdition field-name="partyId" from="customerPartyId"/>`; `SupplierServices.xml:166` `canEditIdentifications:held.PAYABLE_EDIT` and `:228-230` the same find
- **Evidence:** `CustomerFinanceServices.xml:305` `permission:(side == 'supplier' ? 'PAYABLE_EDIT' : 'RECEIVABLE_EDIT')`; `:321` `<service-call name="${existing != null ? 'update' : 'create'}#mantle.party.PartyIdentification"`; `:393` `permission:'PAYMENT_EDIT', side:side`; `:412` `mantle.account.PaymentMethodServices.${paymentMethodId ? 'update' : 'create'}#BankAccount`
- **Evidence:** `PaymentMethodServices.xml:579` `<set field="newPaymentMethod" from="origPaymentMethod.cloneValue()"/>`; `:591-593` an internal owner's method is updated in place; `PaymentRuns.js:79` `if (payroll) methods.sort((a, b) => (b.purposeEnumId === "PmpPayroll" ? 1 : 0) - ...)`
- **Evidence:** `humanres/Employees.js:31` `const taxIdTypes = ["PtidSsn", "PtidFein", "PtidFuid"]`; `:416-426` HR's view `.condition("partyIdTypeEnumId", "not-in", taxIdTypes)` and `valueText: row.idValue ? "•••• " + String(row.idValue).slice(-4) : ""`; `AccountingFinanceDetails.xml:351-354` `<field name="idValue">` ... `<display/>`
- **Not confirmed:** confirmed in code for employees of the same organization; likely for the path across organizations related by `PrtOrgRollup` (whether a child organization's user reaches the parent through the BOSS page: the Mantle path was read, `lib.access` in mr-boss was not). That an existing employee payroll account's `lastUpdatedStamp`, which an update needs, is shown to a treasury user without `RECEIVABLE_VIEW` (`get#CustomerFinance` refuses one without it; `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` has it); whether the `update#BankAccount` SECA that voids open runs' approvals fires for an employee's payroll run. Not run. Looked for and not found: a relationship type condition; an internal-organization refusal such as `PaymentWorkflow.js:35-36` `INTERCOMPANY_EXCLUDED`; a `PAYROLL_VIEW` or People check; masking of `idValue` in the services or the template; an entity filter (every find here is `disable-authz="true"`); a check in the screen beyond the organization (`Receivables.js:245-252`).
- **Suggested fix:** Limit the customer side's relationship to customer relationship types (`PrtCustomer` and the like) and refuse an internal organization and a party with a current employment with the organization; refuse the `PartyIdType`s `PtidSsn`, `PtidFein` and `PtidFuid` (People's tax id types) in these reads and writes, and mask the rest to four digits as People does; leave an employee's bank accounts to the People flow that payroll owns.
- **Re-check:** Confirmed for the scope test and the identification read and write; the lead set the severity to High. Read `account/CustomerFinanceServices.xml:76-90` (with no invoice, order or supplier profile, any `PartyRelationship` in either direction between the organization and the party passes the scope, an employment among them), `:270-277` (every `PartyIdentification` of the party read with authorization off when `canEditIdentifications`) and `:318-322` (one created or updated from the request with authorization off), and `party/SupplierServices.xml:222-232` (the same read for a supplier). Not read by the lead: the relationship types (none is filtered here), the payment method (bank account) write, the rollup path across organizations, and which permission gives `canEditIdentifications`.

#### SEC-BAP-81. A queue's acknowledgement text block runs as server script, with authorization off, for each new case that arrives by mail

High; likely (lead); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-18 (mr-moqui `b9a611246`, `167d04950`; mr-mantle `07ff37bc`, `ce8a8e7f`, `a35e197f`; mr-boss `d0d54714`, `ba547260`, `e60c8789`): `send#IntakeConfirmation` no longer expands the route's text block: it replaces `${requestId}`, `${requestName}`, `${projectName}` and `${queueName}` with their values as plain text, HTML-escaped (`&`, `<`, `>`, `"`, `'`), and any other `${...}` stays as written (`RequestServices.xml`). `save#ServiceRoute` takes as `ackTextBlockId` only the editor's own block, or one shared with a service group of the queue's organization that the editor is in now (`SVO_<organization>`, or `SVT_<team>` of a team the organization owns); a route keeps its present block only when the save is that same route and it is in effect (`request/Channels.js` `ackBlockUsable`, `serviceGroupOf`, `keepsBlock`). ChannelRouting offers the blocks the user may name, each route's form also its own present block (mr-boss `ironstone/customerservice/Channels.js` `withPresentBlock`, `ChannelRouting.xml`), and the demo acknowledgement is shared with `SVO_ORG_ZIZI_RETAIL`. `Security.eecas.xml` `EmailTextBlockScript` requires `DATABASE_ADMIN` to write a `bodyHtml` holding a `${` that is not a plain `${name}` placeholder, so a Comms canned text with another `${...}` now needs it too. No other path expands a text block body. Tests: `mr-mantle/test/request/SupportChannels.spec.js` "a queue's routes ...", "a route names a team's text block only where the editor is in the team's group of the organization; it keeps its own (SEC-BAP-81)", "mail from a customer's contact ... acknowledged once ..."; `mr-boss/test/CustomerService/CustomerServiceChannels.spec.js`; `framework/test/StoredScript.spec.js`.
- **Where:** `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/Channels.js:200-202`, `:546-553`, `:582-594`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:1339-1417` (`send#IntakeConfirmation`); `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/CustomerService/Settings/ChannelRouting.xml:37-45`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/customerservice/Replies.js:66-86`, `:222-228`; `crates/moqui-core/src/script/oxide/resource.rs:221-243`. Report: round 3 `D8.md` (read at mr-mantle `5f5cd403`, mr-boss `4c8d1e6d`).
- **Reached by:** a holder of `SUPPORT_POLICY_EDIT` (the bundle `BOSS_CUSTOMER_SERVICE_ADMIN`, delegable by an organization's access administrator, `BossSetupData.xml:2854`) names the block through Settings › ChannelRouting `saveRoute`, with `ackTextBlockId` from the request. It runs when anyone mails the queue's address from a served customer's address (or follows up a closed case), through the mail rule's `create#RequestFromEmail` and `mailIntake`.
- **What happens:** `save#ServiceRoute` takes any `ackTextBlockId` that exists, with no test of the block's owner or group. The administrator writes a text block of their own (Settings › SavedReplies `saveReply`, through `store#TextBlock`, whose `bodyHtml` is cleaned as safe HTML, which keeps `${...}` text) and names it. On each new case by mail, `send#IntakeConfirmation` disables authorization and passes the block's body to `ec.resource.expand`, which compiles the text as a JavaScript template literal and runs it in the caller's scope with `ec`. The expression runs as the mail intake with authorization off, so it reads and writes any organization's data, a user group membership, the conf. A lower path: a saved reply an agent or team manager shares with a service group is offered in the route's text block options to an administrator of that group (`ironstone/customerservice/Channels.js:139-151`, `:182-185`), so naming a team's reply as the acknowledgement runs that agent's expression. SEC-SCR-06's fix put expression-bearing fields under `DATABASE_ADMIN`; `EmailTextBlock` is not among them. Same class as SEC-SCR-05 and SEC-SCR-06 (record text expanded as script), High as SEC-SCR-05 was.
- **Evidence:** `request/Channels.js:200-202` `const ackTextBlockId = text(p.ackTextBlockId)` / `if (ackTextBlockId && !one("moqui.basic.email.EmailTextBlock", {emailTextBlockId: ackTextBlockId}))` / `denied("TEXT_BLOCK_UNAVAILABLE", ...)`
- **Evidence:** `request/Channels.js:585-591` `if (route?.acknowledge === "Y" && emailAccountId && !acknowledgedEnough(...))` / `call(R + "send#IntakeConfirmation", {requestId, emailAccountId, parentEmailMessageId: p.emailMessageId,` / `emailTextBlockId: route.ackTextBlockId || null,`
- **Evidence:** `RequestServices.xml:1359` `const wasDisabled = ec.artifactExecution.disableAuthz()`; `:1386` `const blockHtml = block?.bodyHtml ? ec.resource.expand(String(block.bodyHtml), '', escapedVars) : null`
- **Evidence:** `resource.rs:236-237` `let (literal, hash) = crate::script::runtime::template_source(&text);` / `match run_here(scope, &literal, hash, super::compiled::FILE_NAME, Some(&text)) {`
- **Evidence:** `framework/entity/Security.eecas.xml` (the expression-field rules) names `LocalizedMessage`, `EmailTemplate`, `EmailTemplateAttachment`, `SystemMessageRemote`, `AuthFlowFieldMap`, `ArtifactGroupMember`, the filter entities and `SubscreensItem`, no `EmailTextBlock`; `basic-comms` `MailSettingsServices.xml:1418` `<parameter name="bodyHtml" allow-html="safe"/>`
- **Not confirmed:** not run. That ammonia's serializer leaves `$`, `{`, `}`, `(` and `` ` `` in text untouched was not read in its source (it escapes `&`, `<`, `>`). The public form path (`RequestServices.xml:943-946`) passes the block too but needs a mail route's `emailAccountId`, so the form does not trigger it. Looked for and not found: a test that the block is the editor's or the queue's organization's; an expansion of named values only (the three variables are passed as a map, but the whole body is compiled); a rule on `EmailTextBlock` writes; a script permission on `save#ServiceRoute` (only `SUPPORT_POLICY_EDIT`, `request/Channels.js:122`).
- **Suggested fix:** Substitute the three variables by name in `send#IntakeConfirmation`, with no `ec.resource.expand` of stored text; in `save#ServiceRoute` accept only a text block shared with the queue's organization's service group; add `EmailTextBlock.bodyHtml` to the `Security.eecas.xml` expression rule while any path still expands it.
- **Re-check:** The lead read `request/RequestServices.xml:1376-1388` (`send#IntakeConfirmation` passes the block's `bodyHtml` to `ec.resource.expand(...)`), `crates/moqui-core/src/script/oxide/resource.rs:228-240` (the text runs as a script template when it holds `$`), and `request/Channels.js:196-202` (a policy editor names any existing `EmailTextBlock` as a route's `ackTextBlockId`) and `:582-594` (it is sent for each new case). Not read by the lead: who may write an `EmailTextBlock`'s `bodyHtml` (the reviewer: not held by SEC-SCR-06's rule), and whether the intake runs with authorization off.

#### SEC-BAP-82. Any staff user writes an upload where their own content-root preference points, through the record files panel and Marketing's campaign and send files, a server script included (SEC-PFS-82's cause; `mantle.content.large.root` is a write root too)

High; confirmed (lead); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-18 (the commits of SEC-PFS-82, one cause): `upload#RecordFile` and the Mantle saves it calls (`save#WorkEffortContentFile`, `save#OrderContentFile`, `save#CampaignAssetFile`, `save#CampaignSendContentFile` and the others) write under Mantle's `mantle@content-root` or `mantle@content-large-root` conf setting, never the uploader's preference (`RecordFilesServices.xml`, `WorkEffortServices.xml`, `OrderServices.xml`, `CampaignServices.xml`, `CampaignSendServices.xml`), and `remove#RecordFile` deletes a stored file only under those roots. The resource facade refuses a write at a location holding `#` on every storage, and an upload's name has its `#` made `_` (`safe_file_name`; `upload#Chunk`, `ClientSyncServices.xml`). Files gives no access to a path with `#` and refuses one in a new name (`get#DriveAccess`, `create#Folder`, `rename#Resource`, `save#Text`, `FileServices.xml`), as do WebDAV and SFTP (`crates/moqui-dav/src/dav/files.rs`). The same class is fixed for the wiki blog image, which goes under the setting with its base name only (`create#WikiBlog`, `update#WikiBlog`), and for drives, whose root is the new `resource-facade@drive-root` setting in place of the `moqui.drive.root` preference (`ensure#PersonalDrive`, `create#Drive`). Tests: `RecordFiles.spec.js` (a task's and a facility's uploads stay under the root with both preferences set elsewhere); `Campaigns.spec.js` (a campaign file under a `withConf` root, not the author's preference; "C# brief.txt" saved as "C_ brief.txt"); `ResourceWriteRoots.spec.js`.
- **Where:** `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/shared/RecordFilesServices.xml:86-90`, `:515-547` (`upload#RecordFile`); `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/WorkEffortServices.xml:1016-1029`, `:1066-1080`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/OrderServices.xml:3164-3183`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/CampaignServices.xml:660-669`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/CampaignSendServices.xml:687-696`; `runtime/components/base/webroot/screen/webroot/apps.xml:15-28`; `crates/moqui-core/src/user/preference.rs:55-72`, `:130-155`; `crates/moqui-core/src/context/resource.rs:530-547`, `:1008-1019`, `:1078-1095`, `:1132-1137`. Reports: round 3 `D9.md`, `D14.md` (read at mr-boss `4c8d1e6d`, mr-mantle `5f5cd403`).
- **Reached by:** any `BOSS_USERS` member (the Commons root's ALLOW, `BossSetupData.xml:111-113`): `POST /apps/setPreference` with `preferenceKey=mantle.content.large.root` (or `mantle.content.root`, which a task or order save takes for a file name the resource facade calls text), then `POST /qapps/Commons/RecordFiles/upload` (`Shared/RecordFiles.xml:59-72`) with `recordEntity`, `recordId` and `contentFile`, on a record they see with no capability needed: one of their own open tasks, an order or a facility of their organization. A self-service employee reaches the same through People's RecordFiles on their own qualification (`BOSS_PPL_FILES_SELF`; `DevelopmentServices.xml:186` names `mantle.content.large.root`, not read further). In Marketing, a holder of `MARKETING_CAMPAIGN_EDIT` on an open campaign or `MARKETING_SEND_EDIT` on a draft send, through the `Marketing/RecordFiles` panel (`Marketing.xml:56-76` admits the campaign and send hosts).
- **What happens:** `upload#RecordFile` admits the record through `get#RecordInfo` (`get#RecordLinkInfo`). For a record type with a Mantle create service it calls that service with the upload; for a facility it writes the file itself. Each builds the location from the caller's preference and writes the upload there with `putStream`: a facility's file at `<mantle.content.large.root>/facility/<id>/content_<row>/<fileName>`, a task's or an order's under `mantle.content.root` (a text file name) or `mantle.content.large.root`, a campaign asset or a send's content under the preference the save picks. As SEC-PFS-82 says, `/apps/setPreference` writes any key for any signed-in user, `get_preference_for` returns the user's own `UserPreference` row before any group row, and the resource facade writes `file:`, `runtime:` and `component:` locations and cuts a component or file location at its first `#`. So a root of `component://mr-boss/screen/Commons.xml#` makes the upload overwrite that screen, and a root with no `#` puts the file anywhere below a tree the process may write. The second key, `mantle.content.large.root`, is a write root too; SEC-PFS-82 counted only `mantle.content.root`.
- **Evidence:** `RecordFilesServices.xml:542-545` `const root = ec.user.getPreference('mantle.content.large.root') || 'dbresource://mantle/content'` / ``const location = `${root}/facility/${recordId}/content_${rowId}/${fileName}` `` / ... `ec.resource.getLocationReference(location).putStream(stream)`
- **Evidence:** `WorkEffortServices.xml:1069-1073` `<set field="contentRoot" from="ec.user.getPreference('mantle.content.root') || ...` / `<set field="contentRoot" from="ec.user.getPreference('mantle.content.large.root') || ...` / `<set field="contentLocation" value="${contentRoot}/work/${workEffortId}/content_${workEffortContentId}/${filename}"/>`; `OrderServices.xml:3174-3179` the same for an order
- **Evidence:** `CampaignServices.xml:663` `<set field="contentRoot" from="ec.user.getPreference(rootPreference) || 'dbresource://mantle/content'"/>`; `:664` `<set field="contentLocation" value="${contentRoot}/marketingCampaign/${marketingCampaignId}/asset_${campaignAssetId}/${filename}"/>`; `CampaignSendServices.xml:690-691` the same for `campaignSend/${campaignSendId}/content_${campaignSendContentId}`
- **Evidence:** `apps.xml:16` `require-authentication="false"`; `:22-25` `ec.user.setPreference(preferenceKey, preferenceValue)`; `preference.rs:63-71` (the user's own row before the group rows at `:73-81`); `resource.rs:1079` `let stripped = strip_prefix(strip_hash(location), &["component://", "component:"]);`
- **Not confirmed:** not run. Whether the server process may write its component and runtime trees in a given install, and whether a changed screen or script is read again at once or after a restart (as for SEC-PFS-82), which decides whether this is code execution or a file write; the upload stream's own `write` (`tools.rs:532`) past the resource facade; whether the multipart parser keeps a `/` or `..` in a file name (D9: the name is reduced to its base name, `multipart.rs:856-858`). Looked for and not found: a fixed root or a root check in `upload#RecordFile`, `save#WorkEffortContentFile`, `save#OrderContentFile`, `save#CampaignAssetFile` and `save#CampaignSendContentFile`; a conf property for either key (`mr-mantle/MoquiConf.xml:8` only names them); a refusal of `mantle.content.*` keys in `set_preference`; a change policy for a task, order or facility in `get#ContentType`. The client upload writes under a fixed `dbresource://mantle/content` (`ClientServices.xml:483`).
- **Suggested fix:** As SEC-PFS-82: take both content roots from a conf setting (or a group preference a user cannot override), never from `UserPreference`, in every service that writes under them; refuse `mantle.content.*` keys in `set_preference`; use the fixed root at `RecordFilesServices.xml:542`; make the resource facade refuse a write location holding `#`.
- **Re-check:** Confirmed for the location. The lead read mr-boss `shared/RecordFilesServices.xml:536-546` (a facility file is written by `getLocationReference(root + '/facility/<id>/content_<row>/<fileName>').putStream(upload)` with `root` the user's `mantle.content.large.root`) and mr-mantle `work/WorkEffortServices.xml:1066-1075` (a task file's root is the user's `mantle.content.root` for a text file name, else `mantle.content.large.root`); with the resource layer's `#` stripping (SEC-PFS-82's re-check) the upload's bytes replace the file the root names. Not read by the lead: the order save and the Marketing saves (D9's reviewer re-read the latter at the lines named).

#### SEC-BAP-83. A store manager's promotion description runs as server script, with authorization off, on every repricing of every order of the store

High; confirmed (lead); fixed.

- **Fix:** Fixed 2026-11-18 (mr-mantle `b78243ab`): the three seeded promotion rules put the promotion's `itemDescription` on the discount line as plain text, or their own default text when it is empty, and never call `ec.resource.expand` (`apply#NewCustomerDiscount`, `apply#BuyGetDiscount`, `apply#FlatDiscount`, `product/PromotionServices.xml`). `store#Promotion` refuses a description holding `${` (`product/StorePromotions.js`), and the entity rule `StorePromotionDescriptionScript` (mr-mantle `entity/StorePromotion.eecas.xml`, the form of SEC-SCR-06's rules) requires `DATABASE_ADMIN` to write one any other way; the field's description no longer says to expand it. No other promotion rule or product text is expanded. Tests: `mr-mantle/test/product/StorePromotions.spec.js` "a promotion's description is plain text: a rule puts it on the line as written, and ${ is refused (SEC-BAP-83)".
- **Where:** `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/StorePromotions.js:395-449`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/PromotionServices.xml:218-230`, `:506`, `:621`, `:705`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Shared/Promotions.xml:43-47`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Shared/PromotionDetail.xml:74-81`; `crates/moqui-core/src/script/oxide/resource.rs:221-243`; `crates/moqui-core/src/script/runtime.rs:554-584`. Report: round 3 `D12.md`.
- **Reached by:** a holder of `COMMERCE_PROMOTION_EDIT` in a store's scope: `BOSS_COMMERCE_MANAGER` ("Store manager", a capability bundle of organization and store scope, `BossSetupData.xml:2252`, `:2918-2920`), `BOSS_COMMERCE_ADMIN` and `ADMIN`, through the Commerce or Marketing Promotions screens (`createPromotion`, `savePromotion`, which call `mantle.product.StorePromotionServices.store#Promotion`). It runs for anyone whose order of that store is repriced: an anonymous shopper applying a code or changing a cart (`ShopCart.js:573-576` calls `add#OrderPromoCode`, which calls `handle#OrderMajorChange`), staff order entry, and the editor themselves as a shopper.
- **What happens:** `store#Promotion` keeps `itemDescription` as typed, trimmed and held to 255 characters. When an order of the store is repriced, `handle#OrderMajorChange` calls `apply#OrderPromotions`, which puts the description into the rule's parameters and calls the registered rule service with authorization disabled. The three seeded rules (`NewCustomerDiscount`, `BuyGetDiscount`, `FlatDiscount`, `MantleSetupData.xml:762-766`) each pass it to `ec.resource.expand(itemDescription, '')`, which turns the text into a JavaScript template literal, copying each `${...}` expression as written, and runs it in the caller's scope, where `ec` is available. So `${...}` in a promotion's description is server script, run on every repricing of every order of the store (for a promotion with no code, every cart change), reaching what a template run reaches (SEC-SCR-81's list: entity finds and writes of any organization, `ec.service.sync()` calls, `ec.service.rest()` and the resource read and write functions). The result is stored as the discount line's `itemDescription`, which the Shop cart shows (`ShopCart.js:346`), so the editor reads the output in their own cart. Same class as SEC-SCR-05, SEC-SCR-06 and SEC-BAP-81, at a field SEC-SCR-06's rule does not hold, written by a store-level role.
- **Evidence:** `Promotions.xml:45-46` `<service-call name="mantle.product.StorePromotionServices.store#Promotion" out-map="saved"` `in-map="{productStoreId:newProductStoreId, serviceRegisterId:newServiceRegisterId, itemDescription:itemDescription,`
- **Evidence:** `StorePromotions.js:413-416` `if (Object.hasOwn(p, "itemDescription")) {` `const description = text(p.itemDescription)` `if (!description || description.length > 255) denied("VALUE_INVALID", ...)` `values.itemDescription = description`
- **Evidence:** `ProductStoreEntities.xml:328-329` `<field name="itemDescription" type="text-medium"><description>Description for the OrderItem (itemDescription), promo service should run this through ResourceFacade.expand()`
- **Evidence:** `PromotionServices.xml:222` `parmMap.itemDescription = storePromo.itemDescription`; `:230` `const result = ec.service.sync().name(serviceRegister.serviceName).parameters(parmMap).softValidate(true).disableAuthz().call()`; `:506` `itemDescription:ec.resource.expand(itemDescription, '')||'New Customer Discount'}"/>` (`:621` and `:705` the same for the other two rules)
- **Evidence:** `runtime.rs:560-562` `if bytes[index] == b'$' && bytes.get(index + 1) == Some(&b'{') {` `if let Some(end) = crate::script::template::scan_expression(template, index + 2, b'}') {` `source.push_str(&template[index..=end]);`
- **Not confirmed:** not run. What a template run reaches was taken from SEC-SCR-81 (round 3 `B9.md`). Who may be given `BOSS_COMMERCE_MANAGER` by invitation or delegation, and the screen authorization of `Commerce/Promotions` and `Marketing/Promotions`, were not traced; other services implementing `apply#Promotion` were not searched for. Looked for and not found: a refusal of `$` or `${` in `store#Promotion`; an entity ECA on `ProductStorePromotion` among SEC-SCR-06's expression-field rules; a rule service that substitutes named values instead of expanding the stored text; a sandbox or locked mode in `expand`; a second person on a promotion.
- **Suggested fix:** Stop expanding stored promotion text: in the three rules use `itemDescription` as plain text, or substitute only named values such as the percent and quantities. Add `ProductStorePromotion.itemDescription` to the expression-field rule of `Security.eecas.xml` as defence in depth, and refuse `${` in `store#Promotion`.
- **Re-check:** Confirmed for the expansion. The lead read `product/PromotionServices.xml:506`, `:621`, `:705` (the three seeded promotion rules write each discount line's `itemDescription` as `ec.resource.expand(itemDescription, '')`, so `${...}` in the promotion's description runs). Not read by the lead: `StorePromotions.js:413-416` (the description written from a `COMMERCE_PROMOTION_EDIT` holder's input) and whether the rule runs with authorization off.
